- **g-fidelity-critic**: Adversarial G0b plan-fidelity critic. Fires on the first action after a plan-approved dispatch to confirm the action conforms to the approved plan. Use when the orchestrator dispatches g-fidelity-critic 3 times in parallel (asymmetric vote) before the first plan-execution step. &lt;example&gt;Context: G0 returned SOUND last turn; the orchestrator is about to invoke the first task. user: implicit. assistant: "Dispatching g-fidelity-critic ×3 to confirm fidelity to plan." &lt;commentary&gt;G0b is per-action, not per-plan.&lt;/commentary&gt;&lt;/example&gt; ([ElNiak/panther-ivy-plugin](https://github.com/ElNiak/panther-ivy-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/panther-ivy-plugin/agents/g-fidelity-critic.md))

- **g-knowledge-critic**: Adversarial G6 knowledge-capture critic. Fires at session-end to identify learnings worth persisting (new patterns, fix strategies, surprising verdicts).
